tbmm-2020-11-04sit03spe0167par0168	Mr. President, dear lawmakers, there have been a lot of questions about the proposal of the law, and I'll try to answer them as long as my time passes. Will Mr. Ali Cumhur Taşkın be given the advantage of paying in advance for the reconstruction? He asked if interest would be paid in installments. The new law is encouraged to pay in advance. The first two installments of the law are on the basis of a discount application. Instead of receiving fertilities such as increases and interest in the amount of the sums being paid during the first installment period, there is a 90 per cent reduction in the calculated domestic HFE rate. Again, a 25 per cent reduction will be made from the originals if there are administrative fines followed by the structured tax agencies. In the case of the first two installments, a 50 per cent reduction from domestic HFE, which has been calculated instead of fiscal gains such as increases and interest, is being paid in the amount of the first two installments. If there are administrative fines followed by tax agencies that you will receive, the originals will be reduced by 12.5 per cent. In addition, the coefficient will not be applied to such payments. The law envisions payment in two ways, in advance or in installments. The debtors will be able to pay their debt by these 2 options, and if they prefer an instalment payment option, they will have to pay extra over a certain coefficient. According to the installments, the coefficients to apply will be 1,045 for 6 equal installments, 1,083 for 9 equal installments, 1,105 for 12 equal installments, and 1.15 for 18 equal installments. Another question from our congressman is, wouldn't the offer be insecurity for a worker working with a fixed-term contract of employment? We all know that the World Health Organization declared a global epidemic on March 12, 2020, depending on the coronavirus, and then a statement was issued to all countries to call for emergency measures. The New Economy Programme 2021-2023 also states that the Covid-19 outbreak will lead to significant losses in labour market supply and employment, particularly in the service sector. The programme said that more flexible conditions are needed to facilitate employment for young people under the age of 25 and employees over 50. In order to protect employment and minimize the impact of the epidemic on the labour market, various regulations have been made, and an arrangement has been required for under-25 young people and workers who are already under 50 years of age to enter the labour market. In this framework, changes to certain fixed-term contract of employment in Business Law 4857, since 2003, were thought to contribute to the labour market and employment of our country during the Covid-19 outbreak. That's a suggestion. According to the proposed amendment, a fixed-term contract of employment can be established for an exceptional two years, without a search for conditions under the age of 25 and over 50. As a result, the proposed change is considered to protect employment and reduce the effects of Covid-19 ▪ on the labour market, flexible the participation of employees in the designated age group. In addition, the arrangement is similar to that of the developed country. The question of Mr. Gaytancıoğlu was, would it not deprive the workers of their right to a fixed-term contract of employment, seniority and reporting compensation? While there are criticisms in fixed-term employment agreements that there is no severance package, it can also be obtained in these cases. The Supreme Court has decided to consider whether or not a fixed-term contract of employment has a desire to renew a certain contract of employment, which appears to be the disadvantage of certain contract of employment, and to be paid severance as a result of the examination of concrete events. There are various decisions made by the Supreme Court's legal offices. The compensation for the complaint is held in the 17th article of the Employment Law, titled "Security of the Work Law," and is found only in the case of uncertain employment contracts. Since the end of the contract is fixed, there is no need for any notification and there is no compensation for a report when the notice period is not met. There will be no change in the matter, since there is no notice compensation in the fixed-term contract of employment. Again, on March 12, 2020, the World Health Organization declared a global epidemic linked to the virus and then issued a statement to all countries to take the necessary measures, calling for an emergency. This framework recommended changes to certain fixed-term contracts in Business Law 4857 since 2003, which were thought to contribute to the labour market and employment in our country during the outbreak of Covid-19. As a result, the proposed change is considered to protect employment and reduce the effects of Covid-19 ▪ on the labour market, flexible participation of employees in the designated age group. The arrangement is also similar to the developed country's practices.

tbmm-2020-11-04sit04spe0177par0072	Mr. President, members of parliament, I have made a pledge on behalf of the National Movement Party Group on the first chapter of Law Proposal 239. I salute the Veterans and our beloved nation. At the beginning of my speech, I wish my condolences to Izmir (Izmir) and to all our citizens who were affected by the 6.6-sized earthquake, the central base of Izmir Segihisar, on October 30th, and to those who lost their lives, Allah's mercy, and to our wounded, and an urgent cure. I beseech the Lord to spare our people all kinds of plagues and plagues. Dear lawmakers, with the proposal of the law that we have discussed, it is primarily intended to reduce the negative effects of the Covid-19 epidemic on employment, to share and lighten the burden on workers and employers due to the epidemic, to support economic activity from the normalisation process, and to maintain employment. This framework envisions extension of the mandate granted to the President on the basis of short work and short work on grounds of covid-19 compelling grounds and the end of normalisation support for the end of the short study, encouraging the job return of those who have been granted unemployment allowance, and providing transfer wage support. In addition, the President is authorized to extend the implementation period to 31/12/2023 if the implementation of support and incentives, which will be implemented by the end of 2020 to contribute to private sector employers and provide employment growth, particularly disadvantaged groups, is required. An exception of income tax and stamp tax for a servant who accepts part-time work is organised for those who sell their products through the Internet and similar electronic environments in their homes. On the other hand, it is intended to change the implementation date to 31/12/2023 of the previous Esnaf Harmonic Ark, which was created for our tradesman, and in the process, there will be no bonus burden on the shop. Another important issue with the proposal is the restructuring of some public debt that concerns many of our citizens. The tax offices, municipalities, municipal private administrations and the finalised purchases of the SGK (SGK) from August 31, 2020 are structured within this scope. All tax dollars and tax management payables include SGK premiums, private administration public fees, municipal water, wastewater, environment, trust rent, pensions, student loans, military, traffic, elections, population and road transport sentences. Judiciary fines, administrative fines issued by regulatory and auditory institutions are excluded from the scope of configuration, as well as fines imposed by Covid-19 for the use of tobacco in illegal places. At the same time, we consider that it is possible for these parts to meet the expectations and ease the burdens of the farmers' debt and loans to our business, agricultural co-ops and forest villagers, as well as the restructuring of their agricultural development co-ops. The calculation of the recipients provides an option to pay on the basis of domestic HFE instead of a delay increase or interest. This framework provides an opportunity to pay for up to three years, with an opportunity to pay 6, 9, 12 or 18 installments per month or every other month. The final application date for use of configuration has been set to December 31st, 2020. Payments to the Treasury and Finance and Customs will begin in January 2021, while payments to the Social Security Agency will begin in February. The first installment you will receive will be waived from the collection of 90 per cent of the amount calculated on the basis of the domestic HFE, provided the entire payment is paid during the first period. As part of the past configurations, those who continue to pay will be able to adjust their remaining debts in accordance with this law. As a result of this arrangement, the Treasury and Finance Ministry's 289 billion and the Social Security Institution's 165 billion-dollar debt configuration. Precious lawmakers are also extended by December 31st 2025 to tax reductions on deposit interest, repo revenues and revenues from private financial institutions regarding securities and other capital market vehicles. Again, the presidency is authorized to reduce the 20 per cent corporate tax rate to 5 points and bring it back to its legal level. It is also provided to reduce the rate of tax on institutions related to companies that are first available to the public. A law proposal also provides an arrangement for the peace of existence. According to this, it is intended to bring money, gold, foreign currency, security and other capital markets into the national economy by bringing them to Turkey. This allows real and legal individuals to freely save their assets within the framework of the provisions in the matter, as they report to the banks and media institutions that brought their assets to Turkey until June 30, 2021. There is also the possibility that there will be legal records of non-results of money, gold, foreign currency, security and other capital markets, which are taxed by income or institutions and are not included in the records of businesses in Turkey. Due to the 2021 UEFA Champions League final match planned to be played in Turkey, some gains and assets are expected to be exempted from income and institutional taxes, and exceptions from the value tax of goods and services. The implementation period is also extended by some regulations that expired on 31/12/2020, and the implementation of the housing tax is envisioned to support the tourism sector by postponing it to 1/1/2022. On the other hand, a career in the presidency is committed to reporting as a profession, and as part of the Constitutional Court's decision to cancel the State of Emergency provisions, additional measures related to those involved are also envisioned to open the administrative application and judicial path. Like the world economy, which has suffered major injuries due to the Covid-19 epidemic, the Turkish economy has been negatively affected by the process. Although the epidemic remains, effective measures have been taken in the economy to turn the wheels and facilitate the lives of our citizens, decisions have been quickly implemented. Economic data has continued to improve since June. The rate of use of capacities is increasing, and the prospect of improving production and employment increases the Economic Trust Index. The growth of the manufacturing industry that has been going on for five months has accelerated in October, compared to the previous month. Despite the conditions of the epidemic, increased orders also increase exports as they support growth. As a matter of fact, in October, our exports reached the highest monthly value of 17.3 billion dollars. Turkey, along with the epidemic, is waging an effective fight with terrorism and external attempts to threaten our rights to sovereignty, which has restored the peace and security of our nation. We believe that our state has both the capacity and the ability to increase the welfare of our citizens without compromise and make our country a leading country. With the legalisation of the law proposal, it will help reduce economic problems and increase employment due to the epidemic, and it will be possible for many of our citizens to pay their debts more easily. With these thoughts, I wish the law's offer to our country and our nation, and salute you worthy members of the General Assembly.

The unemployment benefit of the Fund consists of 1 per cent of the workers, 2 per cent from the employer, and 4 per cent from the state, you know. The Fund's goal is not only to pay those who pay the premiums when they are unemployed; it is also to take increased and protective measures, to improve the quality of the labour force and to do labour market research. This includes payments from the Fund, such as unemployment allowance, short labour allowance, half-work grants, cash allowance, and payment of the Guaranteed Fund. In addition, the employment capacity of the labour force is increased with programmes such as business programmes, vocational training courses, community benefit programmes, internship benefits. These payments are intended for our citizens to be given directly to themselves, to maintain our employees, to improve their qualifications, and to find employment under better circumstances in the following processes. In addition, there are various incentives and supports, such as additional employment incentives, minimum wage support for increased employment and protection, as well as incentives and incentives for employment of women, young and professional certification certificates. Again, these incentives and supports are paid indirectly to our citizens because they reduce the cost of labor and increase the employmentability of people. Moreover, additional employment is essential in the incentives provided by the Fund. Thus, the main goal of these incentives is to protect the employer or to maintain and increase employment, not to transfer funds to the employer. Through the Fund, services offered only in 2002 in line with the needs of the emerging labour market have been diversified and improved quality and quantity while unemployment allowances are being paid only. In this scope, the courses and programmes under active labour force policies, particularly to improve the quality of the labour force, have also begun to use resources for incentives and support. In this framework, the effective use of the Fund (Fon) through active and passive labor practices, considering the socioeconomic structure of its period is a basic priority.
